    directionalLight: BABYLON_2.DirectionalLight;
    dispose: () => void;
    engine: BABYLON_2.Engine;
    ground: BABYLON_2.Mesh | null;
    hemisphericLight: BABYLON_2.HemisphericLight;
    scene: BABYLON_2.Scene;
    startRenderLoop: (onRender?: () => void) => void;
}

}
